# Changelog

All notable changes are documented here.

Format: [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/)

> Domain glossary (Colombian SGDEA — *Sistema de Gestión de Documentos Electrónicos de Archivo*, electronic records management system): **radicado** = an officially registered document with a unique, immutable tracking number; **expediente** = a logical case file grouping related radicados; **TRD** (*Tabla de Retención Documental*) = document retention schedule; **TVD** (*Tabla de Valoración Documental*) = valuation schedule for an accumulated/backlog fonds (documentation already produced without archival criteria, typically from suppressed units or liquidated entities); **dependencia** = organizational unit; **FUID** (*Formato Único de Inventario Documental*) = standard documentary inventory form; **PQRS** = citizen petitions, complaints, claims and suggestions; **índice electrónico** = electronic index that gives the electronic expediente legal validity.

---

## [Unreleased]

### Fixed

- `fix(document)`: **B6 / leftovers — a Salida's origin is limited in one place, resending the same (now inactive) type is not a 422, and import accepts no foreign provenance** — `DocumentService.create` requires, for `doc_type=S` with a human actor, `origin_dept_code` to be a dependency with an active URD of the actor (`422 origen_fuera_de_sus_dependencias`); covers `POST /documents`, quick reply, drafts (duplicate check removed) and each batch item (fails before the sequence). `PATCH` only validates the type when `doc_class` changes. INT-05 moves `from_borrador`/`canal`/`consentimiento` from the ZIP to `metadata.procedencia_origen`.
- `fix(document,auth,tenant,shared)`: **B6 / backend correction round — a record can no longer be born with an invented document type, provenance keys are no longer the client's, and the inert 002 keys leave the allowlist.** *Why*: the B6 audit found that only the public window validated `doc_class` against `tipos_documentales` (via API/MCP a record was born without a TRD series, i.e. without retention), that `canal` (feeds the Law 1581 publication gate) and `consentimiento` (evidentiary consent record) could be supplied in `metadata`, that `from_borrador` was not in the audit entry and the metadata `PATCH` could delete it, that a draft Salida accepted any `origin_dept_code`, and that the parameter allowlist admitted 10 keys from migration 002 that no service reads. *What changes*: **document** — `app/core/tipos.py` is the ONLY place that queries the catalogue (the table lives in the same tenant schema: no HTTP client, like `urd_usuaroldep`): `create` (before `next_sequence`), the retyping `PATCH`, every batch item and the public window (which still also requires `admite_ventanilla_publica`) → `422 tipo_documental_desconocido`; `CLAVES_RESERVADAS = {from_borrador, canal, consentimiento}` is rejected in `DocumentCreate`, `DocumentUpdate`, quick reply and draft, and the system sets them via `procedencia` (the public window no longer sends them in `metadata`; they no longer go through the type's template); the metadata `PATCH` **preserves** them; `document.radicado_created` carries `from_borrador`; draft Salida: `origin_dept_code` limited to departments with an active URD (`422 origen_fuera_de_sus_dependencias`); the draft validates type and metadata BEFORE rendering and uploading (`DocumentService.validar_tipo_y_metadata`), leaving no orphan binary. **auth/shared** — the any-of gate goes through `get_caller` (`make_require_any_permission(identity=)`): 401 without identity and a `usuario_no_aprovisionado` entry, like `require_permission`; the `test_escritura_exige_nivel.py` guardian recognises `require_any_permission(` (an any-of write also requires `min_crud>=2`), with a positive control. **tenant** — the 10 inert 002 keys leave `PARAMS_ADMITIDOS` (`PUT` 422) and move to `PARAMS_INERTES`; the guardian requires seeded = admitted ∪ inert and that no service reads an inert key. *Tests*: each item with mutation (12 mutations, all killed). *Impact*: rebuild and deploy document, auth and tenant; a client sending `canal`/`consentimiento`/`from_borrador` in `metadata` now gets 422. **Pending (roadmap)**: types without an active template store `metadata` unvalidated, and the frontend labels use the ACTIVE template rather than the saved version (`metadata_template_id`).

- `fix(auth,document,tenant,frontend)`: **B6 / `/admin` guardian audit — three admin screens returned 403 to the administrator who does not register, `PUT /config/params/{key}` accepted any key and the frontend guardian could not read any-of gates.** *Why*: the isolation auditor found 0 leaks but 3 UI-to-backend misalignments: `GET /auth/users/pickable`, `GET /email/cuentas`, `GET /plantillas/campos` and `GET /plantillas` required only `PERM_RADI` while `/admin/flujos`, `/admin/grupos`, `/admin/correo` and `/admin/plantillas` open with `USUA_PERM_ADMIN`; and the `PUT /config/params/{key}` upsert stored any key in a table every tenant user can read. *What changes*: `orpycamcp_common.rbac` gains `check_any_permission` (single `= ANY` query, fail-closed) and `make_require_any_permission` (denial audit entry like `require_permission`, `perm_name="A|B"`); auth and document instantiate it as `require_any_permission`. The four reads become **any-of {`PERM_RADI`, `USUA_PERM_ADMIN`}** with no extra exposure (the existing `WHERE` clauses decide the rows; `?todas=true` still requires `USUA_PERM_ADMIN` in the body). `PUT /config/params/{key}` accepts only the 21 keys seeded by the tenant migrations (`422 config_param_desconocido` otherwise); a guardian (`shared/tests/test_params_admitidos_son_los_sembrados.py`) cross-checks them with the seed both ways. Frontend guardian: `gatesFastapi.js` reads `require_any_permission` (each `Gate` carries `anyOf`), `contratoAdmin.js` evaluates it per arm, obsolete exceptions are removed (pickable, email/cuentas, plantillas/campos); `flujos GET /workflows/pending` is relabelled as a scope gate in the body and the `indice/firmar` reason is corrected (the UI already hides the button without both permissions). *Tests*: any-of with mutation (admin without RADI 200, RADI only 200, neither 403, no identity 403, `?todas=true` still 403 with RADI only), `check_any_permission` against real Postgres, allowlist 422/200 and `shared/` guardians (`require_any_permission` counts as a gate; not redefined outside the library). *Impact*: deploy auth, document and tenant (only those three use the new `orpycamcp_common` code; the rest pick it up when rebuilt). `/admin/parametros` still offers a free-form "new key": its `PUT` now answers 422 (left for the frontend).

- `fix(document)`: **B6 / item 23 — registering a draft whose document type has a metadata template with required fields returned 500.** `POST /borradores/{id}/radicar` now maps `MetadataValidationError` to `422 metadata_validation_failed` with `field`, before the sequence number is drawn (no number is spent; the audit entry stays atomic with the radicado). The body accepts `metadata` (`BorradorRadicar`); the `from_borrador` provenance no longer goes through the type's JSON Schema (`DocumentService.create(..., procedencia=)` merges it after validation) and clients cannot send it.

- `feat(frontend)`: **B6 / items 23, 24 and 25 — registering a draft captures its minimum data, stored metadata is read with its template and the receipt error sits on its field.** The register modal asks for document type (catalogue; the template pre-fills only when its type is in it), origin (outgoing; with no active department, registering is not offered) and the type's metadata with `SchemaField`; a 422 keeps the modal open with the error on its field (it used to close before the fetch and lose the input). The proxy forwards a closed list of 4 validated fields; the client rule mirrors `REQUERIDOS_POR_TIPO` with a parity guard against `datos_minimos.py`. New read-only `SchemaValue` in `/expedientes/[id]` (was `[object Object]` and raw key) and in the `/bandeja` detail (it loaded `metadata` and never showed it). `/envios`: the receipt error is tied to the file field (`aria-describedby`, `aria-invalid`, focus) plus 4 new modal cases. `EditarMetadatosModal` and `TransaccionModal` are not migrated: the backend does not allow editing those fields. Tests with mutation.

- `feat(tenant,document,workflow)!`: **batch B5 — the dependencia is correlated by CODE, not by name: renaming with live cases no longer breaks the inbox, `codigo` is immutable and an ambiguous name is never resolved "to the lowest code" (ADR-032).**
  The workflow (`flow_steps.to_dept`, `workflow_rules.assign_to_dept`) and the inbox joined `dependencias` by NAME: renaming a dependencia with live cases dropped its steps out of every inbox and out of the location (tenant-service blocked it with a 409, a divergence from the legacy where everything joins on `DEPE_CODI`); namesakes were confused (`borrador_service` registered towards `ORDER BY codigo LIMIT 1` silently); `codigo` was editable and 8+ tables in 5 services cite it with no FK. Now: tenant migration `013` (immutable-code trigger, name history, single `resolver_codigo_dependencia`), document `046` and workflow `019` (code columns, fail-closed backfill with insert-only evidence anchored in `audit_log`); `PATCH` rejects a code change (`422 dependencia_codigo_inmutable`) and renaming is free; deactivating/deleting are by code (`409 dependencia_con_tramite_en_curso` / `dependencia_con_historia`); new `POST /dependencias/{id}/mapeos-nombre`; APIs take `*_dept_code` and keep the name as a **deprecated alias** (`Deprecation` + `Link`) with `422 dependencia_desconocida|ambigua|inconsistente`; the origin of a transfer is derived by the server (`409 origen_desactualizado`); events carry both fields. Impact: apply tenant `013` → document `046` → workflow `019`; a tenant with live steps of an ambiguous name does not migrate until a manual mapping is registered. Not done: removing the alias (later increment); notification, knowledge, mcp-server, gateway and frontend are a separate batch.
  *Correction round (security and archival audits)*: tenant **`014`** — a manual mapping now IS the answer of `resolver_codigo_dependencia` (it used to be added to the namesakes and never disambiguated), `SET search_path FROM CURRENT` on the function and triggers, insert-only `dependencia_codigo_retirado` (`409 dependencia_codigo_retirado` and a trigger when a deleted code is reused), history seeded from `dependencia.actualizada` audit entries; `referencias()` sweeps `information_schema`; `mapeos-nombre` rejects inactive targets and another active dependencia's current name (irreversible); `PATCH` takes `motivo`. document **`047`** / workflow **`020`**: evidence with `mapeado` linked by `historico_id` and full `antes`/`despues` JSON. Dependencia report: the code-less group gets the fixed label "(sin código)".
  *Last correction round*: document **`047`** no longer changes `resultado` (it rewrote evidence anchored by `046`: the anchor SHA-256 stopped reproducing); it only links `historico_id` and "mapeado" is derived from `historico_id IS NOT NULL` (the `CHECK` is back to the original). `047` and workflow `020` write their own anchor (`document.dependencia_codigo.backfill_047`, `workflow.dependencia_codigo.backfill_020`); `020` only fills new columns, none of those `019` anchors. tenant: `mapeos-nombre` only accepts orphan or ambiguous names (`409 nombre_resuelto_a_otra_dependencia`), `PATCH` rejects renaming to a name already mapped to another code (`409 nombre_mapeado_a_otra_dependencia`); `referencias()` uses `pg_catalog`, counts `workflow_rules.conditions`, and `has_children` runs inside the `DELETE` transaction.
- `fix(archive)!`: **B4 archive re-audit — the delivery package is now the complete delivery and is refused if any component fails its capture fingerprint; the custody trigger only exempts the referential action; a location with a received transfer cannot be deleted.** The package adds the signed electronic index and references the AIP in the note by bucket, key and SHA-256 (not embedded, for volume; the FUID inventory is the frozen act). Missing AIP/index/Object-Lock is `409 componentes_sin_object_lock` (`clasificado/...` paths are aggregated into `componentes_clasificados`, never listed). Before serving: `sha256(acta_fuid_xml)` is rechecked against `acta_fuid_sha256`, the act seal must sign that same act, and each component is read as BYTES and compared with the fingerprint storage recorded at upload; a mismatch is `409 componente_integridad_discrepante` plus a `transferencia.paquete_entrega_incidente` audit entry (`503` if capture fingerprints are unavailable). Tenant migration `067` limits the "update that only nulls" exemption to `pg_trigger_depth() > 1` (a manual UPDATE now raises `23514`); `068` adds a `BEFORE DELETE` trigger on `ubicacion` rejecting (`23514`) deletion of a location referenced by a `recibida` transfer.
- `feat(auth,notification,infra)!`: **batch B4 / P3 — previous COMPLIANCE versions survive a restore, every remapped object gets a PREMIS event, an empty target no longer verifies green and every webhook delivery leaves a record.**
  *What changes*: **infra** — `respaldar_versiones_previas` copies the content of each previous version under live COMPLIANCE or legal hold into `minio-versiones/<bucket>/<sha256(key+vid)>` and lists it in `minio-versiones.tsv` (a separate file; the `minio-retencion.tsv` format is unchanged); it travels inside the encrypted tar; the seal carries `versiones_previas_capturadas=SI|NO` and `backup.sh` exits **5** if incomplete. `restaurar_versiones_previas` (before the `sync`) uploads them by ascending `LastModified`, verifies the sha256, restores retention on the RETURNED `VersionId` and explicitly `put-object`s the current version of those keys; the old→new map includes them. `RESTORE_ACEPTAR_PERDIDA_VERSIONES` remains only for older backups. **auth** — `registrar_restauracion` inserts, in the same transaction as the remap, a PREMIS `REPLICA` event (`detalle.motivo='restauracion'`) per updated row; without `preservacion_evento` everything rolls back. `verify_audit_chains` exits **2** on an empty target unless `--permitir-objetivo-vacio` (which `backup.sh`/`restore.sh` do not pass). **notification** — migration `012` `webhook_entregas` (append-only; no FK to the subscription; body sha256 only) and `GET /api/v1/webhooks/{id}/entregas`; `_deliver_one` writes the `envio` row BEFORE the POST (if it cannot, nothing is sent) and `resultado` after.
  *Not exercised*: `backup.sh`/`restore.sh` end to end against the live store (restore is destructive). Retention/purge of `webhook_entregas`: pending (ADR-018).
- `fix(storage,archive)!`: **B4 / P2 — expediente export is built on disk and served in chunks instead of fully in memory (512 MB cap).** Storage prevalidates the whole inventory with one query, writes the ZIP to a temp file (member by member, 1 MiB chunks, incremental sha256 for `checksums.txt`) and only then answers with `Content-Length`; the temp file is removed in `finally` and a background task, and every failure happens before the first byte (never a truncated 200). New settings `EXPORT_MAX_BYTES` (4 GiB), `EXPORT_MIN_FREE_BYTES`, `EXPORT_TMP_DIR`; `413 export_demasiado_grande` / `507 espacio_insuficiente`. Archive streams without buffering, maps storage 413/507/other to 413/507/502 before auditing/serving, and detects a body shorter than `Content-Length`. Per-radicado no-read-up and the `archive.expediente_exportado` audit entry are unchanged.
- `feat(archive)!`: **batch B4 / P1 — delivery package for the secondary transfer, physical custody enforced in the database, and attempt-numbered PREMIS `dedup_key` (ADR-030 §5).** `POST /api/v1/transferencias/{id}/paquete-entrega` returns a ZIP (admin level 3, neutral `404` no-read-up, `409 paquete_solo_secundaria_recibida`, `409 componentes_sin_object_lock` fail-closed): frozen FUID act as-is, institutional seal and personal signatures, the whole classified chain (constancias, revocations, disclosure envelopes; Ley 1712 art. 18 lit. a), a deterministic `nota-entrega.xml` that references `acta_fuid_sha256` without rewriting the act, and `checksums.txt`. Read and generation audit entries are written in one transaction before the first byte; never exposed through OAI/EAD/CMIS. Tenant migration `065` adds the custody trigger (same rules and codes as the service `422`, `ERRCODE 23514`); `066` adds `fixity_fallos_intentos` so the failed-fixity PREMIS key is `fixity-recepcion-fallo:{id}:{n}`.
- `fix(document,storage,tenant,gateway,shared)!`: **batch B2 — the authorization record's IP is signed by the gateway, the processing policy belongs to each entity and a shipment's receipt belongs to whoever uploads it (ADR-031).** Public-window authorization record (Law 1581) is now **v2** (`ip` only from a gateway-signed header pair, `ip_verificada`, server-computed `politica_huella`/`politica_url`); `GET /public/{tenant}/ventanilla/politica` (strict read, `503 politica_no_verificable`); filing re-reads the policy (`409 politica_desactualizada` / `409 politica_no_publicada`) before uploads, idempotency claim and sequence number; `GET /ventanilla/estado` gains `politica_publicada`; a shipment receipt must be uploaded by the recorder (`403 acuse_ajeno`) and not already referenced (`409 acuse_ya_referenciado`, unique index tenant `045`); storage `POST /internal/files/procedencia`; tenant migration `012` (three empty `politica_tratamiento_*` parameters). `verificar-ventanilla.sh` requires the policy and `seed_demo.py` publishes a demo one. *Impact*: apply tenant `012` and document `045`, rebuild gateway/document/storage/tenant; an entity with no published policy stops accepting online filing until it publishes one; `TRUSTED_PROXIES` must include nginx and the SSR.
- `feat(frontend)`: **online single window with the entity's policy and postal-receipt messages (batch B2).** `/radicacion` reads the policy from `GET /public/{tenant}/ventanilla/politica` (the `POLITICA_TRATAMIENTO_*` variables are removed); the checkbox shows version and link, blocks submission when the entity has no published policy, reloads and asks again on 409 `politica_desactualizada`, and a fixed note links to `#supresion`. `/envios` translates 403 `acuse_ajeno` and 409 `acuse_ya_referenciado`; component test for the receipt modal. *Impact*: rebuild the frontend (needs the B2 backend deployed).
- `fix(workflow,signature,notification,knowledge,shared)!`: **the failed-event queue enforces no-read-up inside the `WHERE`, not as a Python filter over 1,000 candidates (AREA-10-08).** The list used to fetch at most 1,000 rows, resolve their level in Python and paginate in memory, so with more than 1,000 recent restricted rows an old public one fell outside the window and the total lied; the same logic was copied in four services. `orpycamcp_common.deadletter` is now the single source (`LEFT JOIN LATERAL` against the LIVE radicado, `max(live, embedded)`, doubt = level 3) used by list, count, detail, replay and discard; hidden rows answer the same `404` as an unknown `event_id`. `X-Truncated` is gone, a missing `radicados` table answers `503`. Migrations: shared `ix_edl_keyset` index (`workflow 018`, `signature 023`, `notification 011`, `knowledge 013`). knowledge also purges terminal rows older than 90 days and (`014`) keeps `nivel_evento_at` so a stale replayed `reclassified` cannot lower a later level.
- `fix(auth,signature)!`: **`/internal/totp/verify` now requires `X-Internal-Token`, and signing keys maintain themselves.** *Why*: the endpoint that verifies-and-consumes a TOTP was the only auth-service internal route without the service-to-service gate (anyone holding a bearer could burn or lock attempts from the internal network); and the purge of revoked credentials and the KEK re-wrap existed only as an ops tool, unscheduled. *What changes*: `/internal/totp/verify` applies `require_internal_token` (401 missing, 403 wrong, 503 unconfigured; same `SIGNING_KEY_INTERNAL_TOKEN`, no new secrets) and signature-service sends it. New job `app/jobs/signing_key_maintenance.py` in auth-service's lifespan (`SIGNING_KEY_MAINTENANCE_ENABLED`, `SIGNING_KEY_MAINTENANCE_INTERVAL_SECONDS`, default 21600): per active tenant and under `pg_try_advisory_lock`, it purges revoked credentials past `SIGNING_KEY_PURGE_AFTER_DAYS` and re-wraps only rows whose `key_version` is lower than the active one (re-wrap no longer goes backwards if the active version regresses); it writes `signing_key.maintenance_run` with counts when there was activity; without audit it mutates nothing, without a valid keyring it skips the re-wrap. No migrations. *Tests*: 401/403/503 and wire header; integration on real PG with mutations (purge before the window, no audit entry, backwards re-wrap => red). *Impact*: rebuild and deploy auth-service and signature-service together.
- `fix(frontend)`: **the UI mirrors the level-3 requirement for dependencias, catalogs and PINAR writes.** Write buttons/forms (new/edit/toggle dependencia; new/edit/delete catalog item; new plan, approve/run/close, content and instrument editing in PINAR) show only with `USUA_PERM_ADMIN` level >= 3 (`permission_levels` from `/auth/me`); below that the screen is read-only and a `403` is explained in Spanish. Helper `lib/utils/nivelAdmin.js`; test `admin/nivel-admin-escritura.test.js`.
- `fix(tenant,mcp)!`: **writes to dependencias, catalogues and PINAR now require `USUA_PERM_ADMIN` at CRUD level 3 (user decision).** Level 1 (read-only) used to suffice. `POST/PATCH/DELETE /dependencias`, `/catalogos/...` and the 19 `/pinar` writes return `403` at level 1 or 2; reads stay at level 1. MCP tool `registrar_avance_pinar` declares `min_crud=3`. No migrations. Deploy tenant-service.
- `fix(archive)!`: **FIRMA-2, round 3 — orphan envelope purge and on-demand grounds read.** The envelope is now uploaded AFTER the personal signature is validated; if the row never references it (trigger, race, audit failure) it is purged through storage `/internal/purgar-huerfanos`, and a reconciliation sweep picks up `acta-transferencia-sobre-…` files with no reference. `GET /acta` and `/acta/verificacion` no longer return the grounds or salt (nor record a read on screen visit); new `GET .../{excepcion_id}/fundamento?acto=` (admin 3, `via=lectura` entry).
- `feat(archive,storage)!`: **FIRMA-2, round 3 — grounds disclosure envelope.**
  *Why*: the grounds text and its salt lived only in the database (append-only, no Object-Lock, outside the transfer): losing the database lost the grounds entirely (Medium finding of the archival audit).
  *What changed*: every v3 record and revocation now produces an XML `{id, acto, texto, sal_hex, compromiso, algoritmo}` (exclusive C14N), sealed with the institutional seal and WORM-protected with the chain anchor retention (storage `021`: six `acta_firma_*_sobre_<rol>` types, control and multi-artefact; archive `064`: `sobre` artefact in the `_worm` tables, reconciliation/renewal/retry like the other links). No seal: `503` and nothing written. Mediated read only: `GET .../firma-inobtenible/{excepcion_id}/sobre` (admin level 3, `transferencia.acta_fundamento_leido` entry with `via=sobre`). `POST .../revocar` no longer returns the grounds or salt of the original record. ADR-030 documents how it travels in the secondary transfer to the AGN (classified component, Ley 1712 art. 18 lit. a).
- `fix(archive)!`: **FIRMA-2, audit round** — the grounds of the unobtainable-signature record are fingerprinted with a per-record salted commitment `SHA-256(salt || text)` (verifiable by third parties; the salt lives in the database and is shown to admin level 3 only, never in the XML or `audit_log`), what is signed/stored/hashed is the exclusive C14N, `GET /acta/verificacion` checks the chain links (`sustituyeA`, `revocacionSha256`, `constanciaSha256`, stored hash, signed artefact) and a broken link yields `valida=false`, every link of a role's chain shares one WORM retention anchor, `agotado` has an exit (`POST .../worm/reintentar`, admin level 3), `incapacidad` is written `impedimento_sobreviniente`, revocation requires a closed-vocabulary `causa`, and reading the plain grounds leaves a `transferencia.acta_fundamento_leido` entry. Migration tenant `063`; record v3. Response fields `fundamento_hmac*` are replaced by `fundamento_compromiso*`/`fundamento_sal`.
- `feat(frontend)`: **FIRMA-2 in the unobtainable-signature screen**: chain/ordinal, resolved support and people, WORM state of the revocation, required annex, TOTP on declare/revoke, "Verify rationale" button (admin level 3); `/admin/parametros` rejects `null` and holiday writes require admin level 3. Final contract: salted commitment instead of HMAC, mandatory revocation cause, chain links and `cadena_motivos`, WORM "Retry protection" (new proxies), signed-envelope "Download" (admin level 3; new proxy) and the envelope as fourth copy in the grounds check. UX review: on-demand "Show grounds" (admin level 3; `/acta` no longer returns it), the salt is never shown, chain/WORM codes in plain Spanish, focus to the section title after declare/revoke, retry reloads the acta, exception ids validated as UUID in proxies.
- `fix(gateway,signature)`: **FIRMA-2, ronda de auditoría** — the gateway normalizes la ruta (decodifica hasta estabilizar, rechaza `.`/`..`, barras dobles, `\\` y controles) y la reenvía normalizada; bloquea todo segmento `internal` o `*-internal` de cualquier servicio (sin distinguir mayúsculas); los prefijos de ruteo sin barra final exigen límite de segmento. signature retira `POST /internal/hmac-fundamento[/verificar]` (sin consumidor: archive usa compromiso con sal local; evita un oráculo de diccionario) y `PROPOSITO_FUNDAMENTO`.

- `fix(archive,storage)!`: **AREA-07-10, auditor remediation — the "signature unobtainable" record was a note (unsigned, no Object-Lock, free-text support) and becomes evidence: XAdES-T sealed, signed by the declarant per the entity policy, WORM-protected, with a real supporting document, revocable and free of conflict of interest** (ADR-030). Archive migration `061` and storage migration `019`; `firma-inobtenible` now takes `referencia_radicado_id`/`referencia_anexo_id`/`totp_code`, seals before writing (no seal: 503, nothing declared), and a new `.../revocar` act exists; the declarant cannot be any of the three parties; `completitud` gains `sellada_con_excepcion`; `fundamento` is shown only to `USUA_PERM_ADMIN` ≥ 3; verification reads the entity XAdES policy and propagates signature's `advertencias`. Verification: archive 1228 passed/275 skipped (1501 passed/2 failed with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`, a pre-existing state leak between integration files), storage 526 passed/15 skipped, lint green, 31 mutations all caught. Impact: apply storage `019` then archive `061`; rebuild both services; the frontend must adapt the act body.
  *Frontend*: `/transferencias/[id]` declares and revokes the "signature unobtainable" act (radicado/attachment pickers, TOTP per the entity policy, irreversible-act confirmation), tells valid from revoked exceptions apart, and labels `sellada_con_excepcion` as "no party signed; only the entity seal"; allowlisted proxies `POST /transferencias/api/{id}/acta/firma-inobtenible[/revocar]` and Spanish messages for all its 401/403/404/409/422/503.
- `feat(archive,storage)!`: **AREA-07-10 closed — a party of the handover record that cannot sign is declared by an administrative act (level 3, with grounds) and the record becomes "signed with exception"; and the record's personal signatures enter CT retention renewal (user decisions 2026-10-04).**
  *Why*: if whoever had to sign the record died, retired or became incapacitated, it stayed forever without that signature and nothing said so: either left pending with no way out, or signed by someone else (forgery). And the XML of each personal signature had a fixed-date Object-Lock that was never renewed in permanent-conservation series: the lock expired silently.
  *What changes*: **`POST /transferencias/{id}/acta/firma-inobtenible`** (`USUA_PERM_ADMIN` ≥ 3; `rol`, closed-catalog `motivo`, `fundamento` ≥ 20 chars and `referencia_documental` mandatory). Tenant migration **`059`**: immutable record (append-only by trigger and privileges), one per role, `CHECK declarada_por <> parte_id` (the declarant is not the party) and firma↔exception mutual exclusion **in the engine** (trigger with advisory lock) both ways. The party is derived by the server from the frozen record; record + `archive.acta_firma_inobtenible_declarada` entry in the same tx (the entry carries the hash of the grounds, not their text); a 403 leaves a denial entry. `/acta/verificacion` adds `completitud=firmada_con_excepcion` and `excepciones[]`; `firmada_completa` now requires zero exceptions of any role; `GET /acta` carries `excepciones_firma`. `firmar-personal` rejects with 409 before spending the TOTP. Effect on `acta_firma_personal_require`: the exception covers its party (with both covered it is no longer pending); a partial one does not switch the gate off. **CT renewal**: storage accepts `acta_firma_{elabora,entrega,recibe}` in `RenovarRetencionRequest` (no service change: it was already per `(expediente, tipo)` and monotonic); archive, migration **`060`** (state/backoff on `transferencia_acta_firma_personal_worm`), `renovar_worm_firma_personal` and a 4th advisory lock in `run_once_worm_renovacion`; mirror with `GREATEST`; `acta_firma_personal_worm_retencion_renovada` entry; `pendientes-renovacion` lists them with their `tipo`.
  *Not done and why*: (a) the screen to declare the exception and its BFF proxy (contract handed to frontend; `frontend/` untouched). (b) An MCP tool for the act: it is an irreversible administrative act with its own confirmation ladder and was not opened here. (c) The frozen FUID XML is not rewritten (probative, hash anchored): the declaration lives in the record and in `/acta`. (d) The record has no Object-Lock of its own (immutable by engine and backed by the hash-chained entry). (e) The declarant being ANOTHER party of the handover is not prevented: the rule asked is that it is not the party that cannot sign; the admin role bounds it.
  *Verification*: archive-service 1144 passed/267 skipped and 243 passed with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1` (real Postgres: record+entry atomicity, CHECK, mutual exclusion both ways, append-only, CT filter and clearance in the `WHERE`, monotonic mirror); storage-service 463 passed/64 skipped; `./scripts/lint.sh` green on both. 15 mutations (declarant guard, engine CHECK, entry without tx, `firmada_completa` with exception, signing precheck, exclusion trigger, `GREATEST`, 4th phase, CT filter, clearance, grounds text in the entry, seal type instead of the signature's, wrong identity column, storage `Literal`): all fall.
  *Impact*: apply tenant migrations **`059` and `060`** with archive's runner (idempotent); rebuild and deploy archive-service and storage-service. Frontend must add the proxy and screen (contract in the hand-off).
- `fix(signature,tenant,document,archive,docs)!`: **Four user decisions on signing (2026-10-04): the XAdES policy belongs to the entity, ESSCertID is required from a cut-off date, the content is signed rather than the identifier, and an outsider can no longer probe a chain's state.**
  *What changes*: **entity-level XAdES policy** — signature-service reads `firma_personal_require_xades` (`off|clasificados|todos`) from the tenant's `sgd_param_admin` before the signing transaction; the env var is only a fallback when the entity does not define it, and an unknown stored value is applied as `todos` (never relaxes). tenant-service: tenant migration `010_params_firma.sql` (seeds `"off"`) and `PUT /config/params/firma_personal_require_xades` validates the vocabulary (`422 valor_invalido`). Gate behaviour is unchanged: level ≥ 2 without a certificate is `422 firma_xades_requerida`, channel down `503 canal_firma_personal_no_disponible` (never native); `off` keeps the fallback and its `firma.fallback_nativa_canal_caido` entry. The generic `/admin/parametros` screen already lists it. **ESSCertID cut-off** — `TSA_ESSCERTID_CORTE` (ISO 8601, default `2026-10-04T00:00:00Z`; global because the TSA is shared infrastructure) replaces the boolean: `genTime` ≥ cut-off without ESSCertID is rejected; earlier stamps stay valid and `/verify` returns `advertencias[]` (`tsa_sin_esscertid_anterior_al_corte`). **Content, not identifier** — document-service `GET /api/v1/internal/documentos/radicados/{ref}/huella` (SHA-256 of the current PDF) and archive-service `GET /api/v1/internal/expedientes/{ref}/indice/huella` (own router, `X-Internal-Token`): the fingerprint is fixed when the chain opens and re-read before every signature/approval (`409 documento_modificado`; producer down `503`; nothing to seal `409`; chains opened earlier `409 cadena_sin_huella`). **Guard order** — `403 no_es_firmante` before `409 solicitud_no_pendiente` in `firmar`, `rechazar`, `vobo` and `challenge`.
  *Not done*: HSM/KMS (pending a provider; the hook is `auth-service/app/core/signing_keys.py::sign_digest`). *Verification*: signature-service 651 passed/3 skipped with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`; tenant-service 186 passed; document-service 1002 passed with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`; archive-service's 10 new tests pass but its full suite was not finished (`/var` free space below the floor); 22 mutations executed, all caught — mutation M1f (`todos` mode returning "gate inactive") first SURVIVED and was closed with two new `todos` tests at level 1 (422 without certificate, 503 with the channel down); M4c–M4o executed on 2026-10-08 (the original harness was lost; the 13 were rebuilt from their description on read-only copies): 13 of 13 caught (35 of 35 overall). *Impact*: apply tenant migration `010` before signature; export `ARCHIVE_INTERNAL_TOKEN` in production; `TSA_ESSCERTID_OBLIGATORIO` no longer exists; chains opened earlier over a radicado/expediente must be re-opened. *Audit remediation (2026-10-08)*: (1) High — migration `010` seeded `"off"`, which beats the environment fallback `FIRMA_PERSONAL_REQUIRE_XADES`; it now seeds JSON `null` and the new tenant migration `011_params_firma_null_no_off.sql` nulls rows still at `"off"` with no `admin_audit` row for that key (the API always writes one, so an `"off"` the entity chose is kept); idempotent. (2) High — `PUT /config/params/{key}` now requires `USUA_PERM_ADMIN` at level 3, and relaxing `firma_personal_require_xades` requires `motivo` (≥ 10 chars, `422 motivo_requerido`), recorded in the audit entry with `relaja_politica`. (3) `/verify` `advertencias: list[str]` stays an additive contract field (archive-service propagates it). (4) `TSA_ESSCERTID_OBLIGATORIO` present at startup logs an error naming `TSA_ESSCERTID_CORTE`; both new variables documented in `infra/`. (5) the radicado fingerprint is ONLY the `es_principal` annex (`409 radicado_sin_documento` otherwise). (6) `python -m app.ops.invalidar_cadenas_sin_huella` invalidates old NULL-fingerprint pending chains in bulk, one application-written audit entry each. (7) `gate_modo` and `huella_documento_sha256` added to `firma.turno_firmado`; `huella_documento_sha256` to `firma.solicitud_creada`. (8) the 503 for an unavailable producer no longer forwards its body or host. (9) the `GET /signature/requests…` scope (`PERM_FIRMA` + clearance) is declared accepted. *Verification*: tenant 186, signature 651/3 skipped, document 1002 passed (with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`); lint green; 29 own mutations all caught (one survivor closed with a test). *Impact*: apply tenant migration `011`; deploy tenant, document, signature; run the ops script once (`--dry-run` first). Frontend: `PUT /config/params/{key}` takes `motivo`, returns 403 below level 3 and `422 motivo_requerido`.
  *Frontend*: `/admin/parametros` mirrors the `USUA_PERM_ADMIN` level 3 requirement to write parameters, edits `firma_personal_require_xades` with a selector (`null` = "inherited from the environment") and asks for the ≥ 10-character reason when relaxing it; verifications (`/verify`, `/indice/verify`, `/acta/verificacion`) show `advertencias[]`, and the signing-chain 409/503 (`radicado_sin_documento`, `indice_no_disponible`, `anexo_sin_huella`, `documento_modificado`, `cadena_sin_huella`, `verificacion_documento_no_disponible`) are explained in Spanish.
- `fix(shared,auth,tenant,document,archive,storage,workflow,notification,signature,knowledge)!`: **T15 — cross-cutting work on `orpycamcp_common`: the tenant slug was validated in 14 places with five different bounds (and registration accepted `-abc`, which no service accepts), `check_permission` and `resolve_caller_clearance` lived as copies in nine services, and 191 `audit_log` entries said `canal='api'` even when they came from a job** (AREA-02-05, AREA-01-17 and AREA-03-07).
  - T15 audit remediation: tenant-service global migration `003_slug_forma.sql`. It aborts, listing the offending slugs, if `public.tenants` has any slug outside `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{1,53}$` (the shared validator is stricter than the copies it replaced, and such an entity's archive would become unreachable), then adds `CHECK tenants_slug_forma_chk`. SQL<->`SLUG_PATTERN` coincidence guard in `test_slug_unico_contrato.py`.
  *Why*: (1) every `database.py` (plus `keys.py`, `auth_service.py`, `grant_root.py`, `document_repository.py` and the storage repositories) carried its own regex (`{0,54}`, `{0,98}`, `{1,62}` and registration's `^[a-z0-9-]+$`) with no guard checking they agree, and `PreservacionWormRepository` did not validate at all. A future copy without the anchor, or allowing `_`, would permit schema collisions or escape the quoting. (2) The four gate functions were byte-identical (same AST hash) in seven `clearance.py` files and `tenant/authz.py`: a twin diverging from the fail-closed policy in one copy would be an authorization hole no healthy service's tests can see. (3) `audit.append(canal=None)` fell back to `'api'`: purges, WORM renewals, reconciliation and consumers were recorded as API calls, and the legal trail lied about origin.
  *What changes*: **slug** — `orpycamcp_common/tenant.py` (`SLUG_RE = ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{1,53}$`, `slug_valido`, `tenant_schema` raising `ValueError`); the nine `database.py`, `keys.py`, `auth_service.py`, `grant_root.py`, `registrar_restauracion.py`, `document_repository.py`, the storage repositories and the `TenantCreate` schema import it (registration no longer accepts a leading hyphen: 422). **Gate** — `orpycamcp_common/rbac.py` holds `check_permission`, `resolve_caller_clearance`, `nivel_efectivo` and `mensaje_denegacion`; each service re-exports them from its `core/clearance.py`/`authz.py` and keeps only `require_permission`. **Channel** — `audit.append(..., canal: str)` is MANDATORY; `audit.CANAL_PETICION` declares "this act belongs to a request" (inherits the gateway's `X-Canal`, `'api'` if absent) and `"system"` marks jobs, consumers and CLIs; `request_log.canal_de_fondo()` pins `'system'` in each periodic loop's task (archive x3, document x2, storage) and `spawn_supervised` does it for every event consumer, so a mixed function (request and job) records correctly in both. The 191 callers without a channel were rewritten; the internal purge routes (document, storage) and the preservation routes fired by the reconciler (`canal_del_acto(actor)`) record `system`. **`object_ref`** — `GET /api/v1/audit?object_ref=<code>` now also returns the historical entries that name the expediente by UUID or by its index UUID (and a radicado's by its number), resolved at read time with subqueries (no extra round trip); history is not rewritten and the no-read-up trimming applies afterwards. **Docs** — ADR-013 (auto-provisioning is already decided; resolution is now shared), ADR-008, `multi-tenancy.md`, `api.md` and the service CLAUDE.md files.
  *Not done and why*: (a) **permission cache**: still out on purpose (ADR-013: a revoked permission must take effect on the next request; no load profile asks for it). (b) **`require_permission` in `common`**: it depends on each service's `get_tenant_conn`; it stays local. (c) **the `object_ref` history**: not rewritten (hash chain); resolved at read time. (d) **`append_denial`** keeps `canal=CANAL_PETICION` as its default: a gate denial is, by definition, a request's.
  *Verification*: new guards in `shared/tests` — `test_un_solo_validador_de_slug` (tree census + anti-vacuity + parametrized; mutations: reintroducing a local regex and dropping the anchor both fail), `test_el_gate_se_define_una_vez` (mutations: a `check_permission` definition in workflow and `return True` for a null user both fail) and `test_el_asiento_declara_su_canal` (AST census of 190+ calls; mutation: dropping `canal=` from `index_service.py` fails; a background task records `system` even if it declares `CANAL_PETICION`) — `shared/tests` 395 passed; on rebuilt images: document 752 passed/227 skipped, auth 316 passed/44 skipped (plus 3 integration tests of the `object_ref` filter against real Postgres, with the exact-`=` mutation failing; **3 pre-existing failures in `test_verify_audit_chains_job` caused by the local stack's NOLOGIN `orpycamcp_app` role**), tenant 137/7, archive 1084/254, storage 447/64, workflow 355/102, notification 228/20; signature 503/15 and knowledge 262/56 with the code mounted `:ro`; `./scripts/lint.sh` green (ruff + mypy) in all nine services. Live against the stack: `GET /audit?object_ref=EXP-2026-0002` returns 51 entries, including the two naming the expediente by UUID and index; creating an expediente through the gateway records `api`; a background task records `system`; `TenantCreate` rejects `-abc`, `a_b` and 55 characters and accepts `bogota-limpia` (`tenant_bogota_limpia`).
  *Impact*: **no migrations**; all nine services rebuilt and deployed with the local stack's owner role. The internal contract of `audit.append` changes (`canal` mandatory): any caller outside this repository must declare it. Job entries now say `system`; historical ones still say `api`. A 1-character slug or one with a leading hyphen is no longer accepted anywhere (registration already required >= 2).
- `fix(docs,archive,frontend,signature,workflow,storage,document,shared)`: **T16 — documentation and memory: the roadmap contradicted itself about final disposition and kept two closed findings open, `multi-tenancy.md` and ADR-002 prescribed Alembic, SQLAlchemy and an unvalidated `SET search_path`, the getting-started guide told people to onboard an institution through a route the gateway withdrew, ADRs 006/018/021 and the TSA comments said "no hard barrier", "recording deliveries" or "stub" about things that already exist or never existed, the EN roadmap was 18 open items behind the ES one, and the per-unit loans listing returned the whole history**
  *Why*: every false statement was written when it was true and the code moved on; a text saying "not implemented" about something that exists reopens what is closed, and an unsafe recipe in an ADR gets copied by whoever reads it. The audit items (AREA-14-01/-02/-03/-04/-06/-07/-08/-09/-10/-11/-13/-14/-15/-16/-17/-19, AREA-02-01, OMI-07, AREA-07-19, AREA-07-16) are detailed in the Spanish changelog.
  *What changes*: **docs** — roadmap ES/EN (final disposition becomes history with the current state; the closed findings carry their evidence and the accepted residuals: invalid tokens are not cached, the limiter fails open without Redis; header dated 2026-10-03, no hand-written ADR count; the 18 stale EN open items closed with their reason); `multi-tenancy.md` ES/EN rewritten over the real pattern (`tenant_schema` with a fail-closed `_SLUG_RE` + `SET search_path = "{schema}", public` on asyncpg; onboarding through `scripts/init-tenant.sh`; the shared stream `orpycamcp.{service}.events`; `documents` and `preservation` buckets); ADR-002 gains a "partially superseded by ADR-003 and ADR-027" note with the safe snippet; ADRs 006/016/018/021 corrected without touching their decision; `deployment.md` downgrades Kubernetes to "not supported yet" and warns about in-memory state when scaling; `DOCKER_REGISTRY.md` rewritten for GitLab; `getting-started` no longer curls `/api/v1/tenants`; `security-audit.md` EN gets the second-pass audit and the citizen-window behaviour change. **code (comments only)** — TSA, workflow, storage, OAI and transfer-repository comments, two obsolete frontend TODOs. **signature** — tenant migration `022` (`COMMENT ON COLUMN firma.xades_level`). **archive** — `GET /unidades/{id}/prestamos` paginates (`page`/`size` ≤ 100, `X-Total-Count`) and the frontend BFF requests `size=100` and keeps the total; `tests/test_ead_xsd.py` validates the disseminated EAD (no series, 1, 2, 3 and 5 levels) against the official EAD 2002 XSD vendored in `tests/fixtures/`. **shared** — `test_la_documentacion_dice_lo_que_hay.py`: 20 guardians tying each sentence to the code fact that would make it false.
  *Not done and why*: the durable `webhook_entregas` table (ADR-018 now states the truth and declares it pending), EN changelog parity (the project documents the English changelog as a selection, not a translation: open question), a generated MCP-coverage figure (needs the eleven OpenAPI schemas live; the split between "excluded on purpose" and "real gap" is the owner's call), a level discriminator for the CCD (`trd_series` has no level column, ADR-015), export streaming and a load suite (own batch). AREA-14-12 turned out to be closed by migration `028`, which already rewrote that `COMMENT`.
  *Verification*: documentation guardian 20 passed (27 mutations reintroducing each false sentence: all fail); archive-service 1084 passed/254 skipped (+ real-Postgres integration); signature-service 503 passed/15 skipped; ruff+mypy green on the five services touched. **Images were not rebuilt** (`/var` at 98 %): tests ran on the existing image with sources mounted read-only.
  *Impact*: deploy archive-service and the frontend; signature-service applies `022` (a `COMMENT` only); the rest is comments and documentation.
- `fix(infra,auth)`: **T14, audit remediation: a single broken `audit_log` row aborted every backup forever (leaving a clear-text `.PARCIAL` each night), unread retention was taken as "no retention", previous COMPLIANCE versions were lost with a warning and rc 0, restoring left no immutable trace, and production services carried the owner connection in their environment** (1 High + 3 Medium; the fourth Medium, ungated `knowledge`, stays declared).
  *What changes*: `verify_audit_chains` accepts a responsible-approved baseline of historical breaks (`--roturas-declaradas FILE`, or `ROTURA<TAB>slug<TAB>id<TAB>note` lines in the anchor stdin): only NEW breaks, non-enumerable breaks, a count regression or a changed anchored hash count. `backup.sh` no longer aborts: on a failed verification it emits no anchor but completes (encryption, seal with `verificacion_auditoria=FALLIDA`, purge) and exits **3** at the end; baseline via `BACKUP_AUDIT_ROTURAS_DECLARADAS`. Retention reads distinguish "no configuration" from any other failure; a failure aborts that bucket, listed-versions vs captured rows must match, the backup is sealed with `retencion_capturada_completa=NO` and exits **4**, and `restore.sh` refuses it unless `RESTORE_ACEPTAR_RETENCION_INCOMPLETA=1`. A previous version under live COMPLIANCE now fails the restore unless `RESTORE_ACEPTAR_PERDIDA_VERSIONES=1`; the new `app.ops.registrar_restauracion` updates `preservacion_worm_objeto.version_id` from the map and writes the `storage.restauracion` audit entry in ONE transaction. In production `MIGRATIONS_DATABASE_URL` (owner) leaves the 10 running services and stays only in `migrate-<svc>`; the guardian was inverted.
  *Not done*: gating `knowledge /search`, `/antecedentes`, `/rag` (product decision, stays declared); a PREMIS event per remapped object. *Verification*: `shared/tests` 333 passed; auth-service 362 passed with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`; lint green; mutations fall as listed in the Spanish entry. *Impact*: rebuild auth-service; `backup.sh` may exit 3 or 4 with a complete sealed backup (treat as alert); production runtime can no longer run migrations with `run <svc>`: use `migrate-<svc>`.
- `fix(infra,auth,ci)!`: **T14 — CI and infrastructure guards: no non-negotiable rule had an executable guard, the CI matrix was a hand-written list (no signature in integration, no whisper in unit), production built on the server with no way back and no migrations at startup, the backup ANCHORED the audit chain without verifying it (and the `restore.sh` heredoc blew up with a `TypeError`), the restore accepted an unrestored retention as fine, and the dependency audit could not fail** (AREA-01-18, 13-01/02/03/05/08/09/11/13/14, 08-10/12, 03-02/05/06, 05-08).
  New executable guards in `shared/tests` (every push): every route carries a gate or a declared exception with its reason (AST census of over 400 routes; knowledge's `/search`, `/antecedentes`, `/rag` and `aceptar` are DECLARED, not approved — gating them is a product decision), no ORM/`python-jose` and the version floors, repo bind mounts read-only, CI matrices derived from the tree, immutable images and migrations-before-serving in the production manifest, a safe production manifest (no `ports:`, Redis password, mandatory credentials, pinned third-party images). CI: whisper in `test-unit`, signature and workflow in `test-integration`, the real auth-service global migrations before each integration tier (the integration conftests now FAIL under `CI` instead of creating their own copy of the `audit_log` DDL), a `storage-worm-real` job (RustFS pinned by digest; it runs when a compose file changes), an automatic `test-security-smoke`, Trivy before every image push, a fail-closed `dependency-audit` with a deterministic threshold (`scripts/audit_umbral.py`; the model only drafts the report) and an `npm-audit` job. Production: `image: ${ORPYCA_REGISTRY:?}/<svc>:${ORPYCA_TAG:?}` for the 12 first-party services (no `build:`; local builds live in `infra/docker-compose.build.yml`) and a one-shot `migrate-<svc>` per database-backed service. Backup/restore: `auth-service/app/ops/verify_audit_chains.py` is invoked by `backup.sh` BEFORE anchoring (no anchor if it fails) and reused by `restore.sh`; `infra/lib-retencion-s3.sh` saves every version and the legal hold, restores and COUNTS (`restore.sh` exits non-zero if a valid retention or legal hold is left unrestored) and writes the old → new `version_id` map for the ADR-027 record. auth-service's `/health/ready` adds `audit_particiones` (`degraded` if next month's partition is missing; still 200). `scripts/preflight-prod.sh` checks TLS and ports from outside. `seed_demo.py` seeds a draft with a two-turn circuit whose text changes inside the invalidation window, and `firma-gate-orden.spec.js` exercises the order gate.
  Not done: gating knowledge's routes; migrations on startup in the development compose; an entrypoint inside the 12 Dockerfiles; previous versions with retention cannot be restored (reported). **Impact**: production needs `ORPYCA_REGISTRY` and `ORPYCA_TAG` on every `docker compose`; rebuild auth-service (it was NOT rebuilt on the local stack: `/var` had ~460 MB free); no new SQL migrations.
- `fix(document,frontend)!`: **T13 — document compliance: a radicado was born with no type and no department, the classified-information index admitted NULL legal bases, a level change was published after the commit and lost with Redis down, the public window kept no proof of the personal-data authorisation and had neither an automation brake nor a safe retry, the postal webhook secret was single and in clear, and publishing ex officio disclosed the first attachment** (AREA-09-02/04/05/07/08/10/16, 07-12 and 02-07 — the latter only the honeypot and the `Idempotency-Key`).
  *Why*: (1) `DocumentCreate` and the batch left `doc_class`, `dest_dept_code` and `origin_dept_code` optional (AGN 060/2001 §5.2): a radicado could be born with no TRD series and no destination, in nobody's inbox. (2) The INT-05 import accepted `nivel ≥ 2` with a null `clasificacion.fundamento` and the database did not prevent it. (3) Reclassification published its event after the commit and swallowed the failure: with Redis down right then, the level stayed raised and knowledge — which derives its fragments' level ONLY from the event — kept serving the fragment at the old level (R6, declared and deferred). (4) The single window and PQRS registered the sender with no proof of consent (Law 1581), no trap field and no idempotency key: a network retry spent two official sequence numbers. (5) `postal_operator_credential.webhook_secret` was clear TEXT, one row per operator, no endpoint wrote it and there was no rotation; a shipment could be moved to `entregado` by hand with no evidence. (6) `get_public_anexo` served the first attachment of any published radicado, and publishing only required `nivel 1` (the DEFAULT of a PQRS). (7) `documento.contenido_cambiado` was never emitted.
  *What changes*: **minimum data** — `app/core/datos_minimos.py` (E/I → type + destination; S → type + origin) imported by `DocumentCreate` **and** `BatchDocumentCreate` (the batch gains `origin/dest_dept_code` and resolves the name in `dependencias`); internal producers **derive or reject before spending a number** (`exigir_o_422`): the quick reply inherits origin and type from its antecedent, a radicado from a draft derives type (template), destination (name) and origin (issuer's URD) and `POST /borradores/{id}/radicar` takes an optional body that wins; the mailbox intake requires it before fetching the message. **Index** — migration `041` (`CHECK radicados_clasificado_con_fundamento` `NOT VALID` + "PENDIENTE DE MOTIVAR…" backfill + `VALIDATE`; `create` now inserts the basis because the CHECK cannot be deferred), the import rejects the whole package (422) and the index adds `dependencia` and `registrado_por`. **Outbox** — migration `042` (`eventos_salientes`) + `services/outbox.py`: creation, reclassification and annulment enqueue the event in the SAME transaction as the change and its audit entry; fast path after the commit and a relay in the sweep (phase 4, `SKIP LOCKED`), same `event_id` on resend; `BorradorService.editar` emits `documento.contenido_cambiado` when the text fingerprint changes. **Publication** — migration `040` (`anexos.publicable`, DEFAULT FALSE, backfill of what was already published), `PublicacionRequest.anexos_publicables`, `get_public_anexo` requires `a.publicable`, and `set_publicable` excludes in the `WHERE` anything with channel `ventanilla_publica` or typed with a `admite_ventanilla_publica` type (neutral 404); classifying unmarks attachments. **Public window** — `acepta_tratamiento: Literal[True]` + `politica_version` (proof in `metadata.consentimiento` and the audit entry; `ip_declarada`), honeypot `sitio_web`, `Idempotency-Key` with `public_submission_keys` (migration `043`, claimed inside the radicado's tx and before the sequence number; other content → 409), `GET /ventanilla/estado` and `scripts/verificar-ventanilla.sh`; the SvelteKit form carries the checkbox (with the entity's policy: `POLITICA_TRATAMIENTO_VERSION/URL`), the off-screen trap and one key per content. **Postal** — migration `044`: two encrypted secret slots (AESGCM, AAD entity+operator), `verify_any`, `PUT /envios/operadores/{op}/credencial` (`USUA_PERM_ADMIN` crud≥5, never returns or audits the secret), `acuse_file_id/acuse_sha256` mandatory for a manual `entregado` (the shipments screen uploads the receipt and sends it). **Storage** — `REFERENCIAS_DE_ARCHIVO` += `postal_shipment.acuse_file_id` (the guardian `test_referencias_de_archivo_cubren_todo_file_id` caught it: without the entry the orphan purge would not see the receipt's reference); the guardian `test_eventos_de_radicado_declaran_nivel` now also censuses `outbox.encolar` (otherwise the emitter census would silently empty out) and `documento.contenido_cambiado` declares `nivel_seguridad`. **Docs** — the R6 comment in `knowledge-service/app/services/ingestion.py` corrected (closed at the source).
  *Not done and why*: (a) **the CAPTCHA provider (AREA-02-07)**: user decision (reCAPTCHA contradicts sovereignty; ALTCHA or another self-hosted proof of work, verified in document-service before opening the transaction). (b) **not binding an anonymous submitter to an existing third party's record by document number (AREA-09-08 note)**: it changes the directory convergence the tests fix today; question: should the anonymous channel register with `metadata.remitente` and no record when the document already exists in the directory? (c) **series/file level of the index and a closed catalogue of legal bases (AREA-09-04.3)** and **double control for declassification (AREA-09-05)**: entity decision. (d) **fragment-level reconciliation sweep in knowledge (AREA-09-05)**, **a `document.envio.devuelto` consumer in notification (AREA-09-10)** and **a `documento.contenido_cambiado` consumer in signature (AREA-09-16)**: outside this batch's services; the outbox closes the loss at the source and the synchronous signing guarantee already exists. (e) **`tipos_documentales.publicable_permitido` (AREA-07-12)**: an archive-service column; `admite_ventanilla_publica` was reused, which already is the entity's declaration of "this comes from outside"; publication history stays in `audit_log`. (f) **IP verified by trusted hops on public routes**: the gateway does not inject it; `ip_declarada` is stored. (g) **`habilitada` inside `GET /ventanilla/tipos` (AREA-09-07)**: it would have broken the response shape (a list) and an empty list cannot carry it; it is a new endpoint `GET /ventanilla/estado`. (h) **type/department capture in the drafts form**: without it a draft with no template type and no author URD answers 422 on radicar (the optional body solves it by API).
  *Verification*: document-service 741 passed/219 skipped and **219 passed with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`** (real Postgres: 23 new tests — CHECK 041 and its backfill, the publication `WHERE` by type and by channel, `anexos.publicable`, the outbox with Redis down, the UPDATE rolling back if the event INSERT fails, the idempotency key under `asyncio.gather` over 4 connections, the draft derivation against the real tables, the batch with a missing department); frontend 2655 passed (235 files; 8 new tests: the checkbox, the hidden honeypot, the per-content key, the proxy and the receipt); ruff+mypy green (storage-service: 447 passed/63 skipped and ruff green; `shared/tests` 255 passed). **17 mutations that fall** (all restored, image rebuilt): dropping the E branch / `doc_class` from S / the batch validator / the import check / CHECK 041 / `AND a.publicable` / the type / channel exclusion in the `WHERE` / moving `encolar` out of the tx / moving the key claim out of the tx / the honeypot / `Literal[True]` / a constant `habilitada` / the previous slot's expiry / the mandatory receipt / the `enviar` of `contenido_cambiado`. **Verified on the live stack (tenant `demo`)** end to end through the gateway and the frontend proxy: the 422/201 matrix of minimum data, the batch with a missing department, the index fields and the CHECK, the outbox fast path and a relay resend (same `event_id`), `ventanilla` (enabled only for the test and put back) with consent, honeypot, the same `Idempotency-Key` sequentially and with **three simultaneous requests** → one radicado and one sequence number, 409 on other content, publication by attachment and the exclusions, the HMAC rotation with the previous slot valid then expired (401) and the secret never in clear, `entregado` with and without the receipt, `documento.contenido_cambiado` in the stream, and `radicar` from a draft with and without data. **Disk incident during deployment**: rebuilding the frontend (its `npm install` cache had been pruned) and ~500 MB of WAL from the integration runs took `/var` to 0 and Postgres went down with a `PANIC` for lack of space; space was freed (WAL via `pg_switch_wal`+`CHECKPOINT`, `max_wal_size` lowered with `ALTER SYSTEM` to 64 MB and `min_wal_size` 32 MB — reset with `ALTER SYSTEM RESET` —, an unused `python:3.12-slim` image and the container-less `latest` images of tenant, notification, workflow, signature and auth, all rebuildable) and `orpycamcp-postgres` was started again (recovered with no loss).
  *Impact*: **breaks clients that register without type/department** (`POST /documents`, `POST /batch/documents`, the single window and PQRS without `acepta_tratamiento`/`politica_version`, a manual `entregado` without a receipt): warn integrations; a batch queued before the change without codes ends with those items `failed`. **Migrations `040`–`044` per tenant** (document-service's `python migrations/runner.py`; no cross-service dependency: `tipos_documentales` already existed from archive-service), rebuild **document-service, frontend and storage-service** (the latter a one-liner: `REFERENCIAS_DE_ARCHIVO`). Rotating the postal secret needs `EMAIL_ENCRYPTION_KEY` (otherwise `503`). Publishing a radicado no longer publishes its attachments: mark them with `anexos_publicables`. Other services' integration tests that seed `radicados` with `nivel_seguridad ≥ 2` through raw SQL must carry a basis.
- `fix(document,knowledge)`: **T13, audit remediation: the outbox relay re-sent an old reclassification with its original level, the delivery receipt was recorded without checking it against the object store, and the 041 backfill left no audit entry** (AREA-09-05, 09-10 and 09-04).
  *Why*: (1) `retransmitir` re-sent any pending row older than 60 s without checking whether a later reclassification of the same radicado had gone out; knowledge sets the EXACT level, so a late A (3→1) after a B (1→3) lowered the chunk and served it to people who should not see it. (2) `acuse_file_id`/`acuse_sha256` were only shape-checked: an invented UUID marked the shipment «delivered» and was cited as proof. (3) The 041 `UPDATE` changes the Law 1712 art. 20 index field with no trace (invariant 6).
  *What changes*: the relay publishes the CURRENT `nivel_nuevo` read from `radicados.nivel_seguridad` in the same transaction (`outbox._con_nivel_vivo`; the event contract is unchanged). `POST /envios/{id}/estado` checks the receipt against storage (`/internal/files/hashes`, which only knows the tenant's files) before the transaction: `422 acuse_invalido` if the file does not exist or the hash differs, `503 acuse_no_verificable` if storage is down, and the hash returned by storage is the one recorded. The migration runner (`asentar_backfill_fundamento`) writes a `document.clasificacion_fundamento_backfill` audit entry (actor `system:migration-041`, `de: null`, `a: <text>`) for each row the 041 filled, idempotent and valid where 041 already ran; it lives in the runner, not in SQL, because the hash chain is computed by the application. Fixed the comment in `knowledge-service/app/services/ingestion.py`.
  *Not done and why*: (a) comparing `occurred_at` in knowledge as a second net (needs a column and migration in another service; the source no longer publishes stale levels). (b) requiring that the receipt was uploaded by whoever marks the shipment, and rejecting a `file_id` already used as an attachment: storage does not expose that per file and the cross-reference lives in another service; left as an improvement. (c) the rows filled by the 041 require the entity to give their grounds: the text «PENDIENTE DE MOTIVAR» appears verbatim in the index.
  *Verification*: document-service 979 passed with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1` (real Postgres; 4 new unit and 4 new integration tests) and mutations that fail: removing `_con_nivel_vivo`, skipping the receipt check, removing the backfill's `NOT EXISTS` or `nivel >= 2` filter; shared/tests 255 passed; ruff and mypy clean in document-service and knowledge-service.
- `fix(archive,storage)!`: **T12 — archival compliance: the index was signed without validating its XSD, a transfer was prepared with an unsigned index and its record did not say which signature covered it, accumulated holdings and the record's personal signatures had no Object-Lock, the AIP replica was never re-read, and annex deduplication revealed reserved annexes** (AREA-07-01/03/04/05/06/08/09/11/14/15, 06-04/07, 08-02/04/05/09/15; **07-10 only the technical WORM**). **archive**: `index_xsd.py` (lxml) validates the index before sealing, on close, manual retry **and** reconciliation (`pendiente_firma`/`xsd_invalido`, never signed) and exhausted sealing sends an email to whoever can repair it; `create` requires the signed index (`409 indice_no_firmado`), reads custody inside the transaction with `FOR SHARE` and rejects a pair with no custody (`422 ubicacion_sin_tenencia`); migration `053` ties `indice_firma_id`/`indice_sha256` into the handover record (columns and the hashed XML) and `cabecera_completa` is derived on read; `054` revokes UPDATE/DELETE on the record from the app role, `055` adds a `CHECK` on `objeto` (history exempt: a bare `NOT VALID` is also evaluated on each UPDATE), `056` records the receipt's fixity check as a PREMIS event (post-commit, idempotent, reconciled), `057` gives each personal signature WORM wiring, `058` + a job complete legacy producer/format/size on **open** expedientes only (NULL columns only); accumulated holdings are wired to WORM with the extreme date and a clamp (`today + 400 days`, ADR-026 Inc.3); `POST /expedientes/{id}/aip/reintentar`; preservation events now include the AIP and per-file ones; `PATCH /trd/{id}` accepts both phases and the legacy set is derived. **storage**: deduplication is for the same author only (no more existence oracle), the AIP replica is re-read (retention + SHA-256) with a sanitised failure reason, startup guard for veraPDF with no binary, `looks_like_pdf` tolerates a 1 KiB prefix, PREMIS declares the PRONOM PUID, migration `018` (`acta_firma_*` types), `POST /preservacion/eventos-internal`, and a WORM-circuit test against real Postgres + RustFS. Not done: the `firma_inobtenible` act (07-10) and the two-level TRD hierarchy (06-07) are user decisions; `preservationLevel`/`rights` in PREMIS; a notification consumer for "index exhausted" (it is a direct email today). **Deviation to confirm**: the accumulated-holdings WORM uses a ~400-day clamp instead of "set no retention" (ADR-026 D4.4) — an irreversible COMPLIANCE over documentation whose schedule already expired. Verified on the live stack (preservation temporarily enabled, then restored): a 1985 holding ended under COMPLIANCE until exactly `today + 400 days`. Impact: rebuild storage and archive (adds `lxml`; watch disk); migrations in order — storage `018`, then archive `053`–`058`; census before deploying to a real tenant: closed expedientes with index ≠ `firmado` and active transfers (the local stack had 0 and 0).
  *T12 audit remediation (2026-10-03, archive)*: a fondo acumulado rejects a future `fecha_extrema_final` (`422`; `base_worm` returns `None` fail-closed); a failed receipt fixity now records a PREMIS `FIJACION` event with `resultado="fallo"` (counts only), and a fixity with files lacking a hash is declared `verificacion="parcial"`; `archive.vinculo_origen_completado` carries the written `valores`, `fuente` and timestamp, and the backfill takes `FOR SHARE` on the expediente row to serialize with closure.
- `fix(frontend,storage,gateway,document,archive)`: **T11 — BFF guards, cookies and RBAC mirror** (AREA-12-01/03/04/05/06/07). A tree-derived guardian requires a session on all 290 verbs of the 219 `(app)` `+server.js` (was 21 hand-picked); a test asserts the session-cookie flags; `admin/+layout.server.js` redirects to `/403` by each area's real permission (fail-closed `USUA_PERM_ADMIN` for undeclared areas); `GET /api/v1/preservacion/estado` (instance state, no per-expediente read) lets the Preservation tab say "disabled on this installation"; `SchemaField` supports arrays of scalars and one-level objects, and the 422 `metadata_validation_failed` envelope now carries `field` (document-service and archive-service); `layout.scss` uses `--op-scrim` and the scrim guardian covers `.scss`; PINAR gains three missing BFF doors plus a backend↔BFF parity guardian (the "36 endpoints, UI consumes 6" figure was false: 30). - `fix(knowledge,mcp,gateway,shared,infra)!`: **what the assistant confirms was recorded as channel `api` with no way to tie it to its turn or proposal; the assistant did not check that the radicados in its text exist; all its calls shared one container's per-IP bucket; `ollama_local` was "sovereign" by name even with a public `OLLAMA_URL`; RAG suggestions had no human acceptance; and "per-tenant opt-in" was a false statement**
  *Why*: (1) everything the mcp-server does on behalf of a person enters the gateway through `/api/v1/<domain>`, and `derive_canal` only knew `mcp` by PATH: a radicado created from the card ended up `api`, like a direct call; and even with the gateway saying `mcp`, `audit.append` defaulted to `canal="api"` and document-service did not pass it, so the channel was lost one hop later (AREA-11-04). (2) "do not invent numbers" was one line of the prompt: nothing checked the final text (AREA-11-04). (3) the mcp-server did not forward the client IP: the assistant calls of ALL entities fell into its container's per-IP bucket, the NAT problem of `9c2cc6a` one hop further in; and a turn's cost was not measured (AREA-11-08). (4) `is_generatable` decided by the NAME `ollama_local`; with a public `OLLAMA_URL`, `/rag` sent level 2-3 chunks to that host and left only a warning (AREA-11-12). (5) `kb_suggestions.aceptada_por/aceptada_en` existed and no endpoint wrote them (AREA-11-11, CC-02). (6) `main.py`, the guide and ADR-006 said "per-tenant opt-in": it does not exist, ingestion is global (OMI-04).
  *What changes*: **shared** `identidad.firmar_origen/verificar_origen` (mark `X-Origen-Canal: mcp.<ts>.<hmac>` bound to the tenant and the sha256 of `Authorization`, 120 s validity, with no secret it is neither issued nor verified) and `ip_real_firmada` (`X-Real-Ip` only if the accompanying identity verifies); `request_log` captures `X-Canal` (only `api|ui|mcp`) and `Idempotency-Key` into ContextVars; `audit.append`/`append_denial` take `canal` from the request when not passed and, **only on channel `mcp`**, add `payload.correlacion = {request_id, idempotency_key}`. **api-gateway** `derive_canal(path, origen, tenant=, authorization=)`: `mcp` by path or valid mark, `api` otherwise (the mark is not forwarded); forwards `Idempotency-Key`; routes `/api/v1/knowledge/suggestions/`. **mcp-server** `services/origen.py::cabeceras_de_origen` is the single place that builds outgoing headers (dispatch, `/auth/me`, scope frame): `Authorization`, `X-Tenant-Slug`, the mark, `X-Request-Id` and `X-Forwarded-For` with the IP the gateway signed; `ContextoEntranteMiddleware` + `RequestLogMiddleware`; the proposal `jti` travels as `Idempotency-Key` (in a procedure, `<jti>:<n>` per step); `run_turn` returns `no_verificados` (every `{YYYY}-{TENANT}-{E|S|I}-{NNNNNN}` in the text absent from the `tool_result`s; what the person typed does not count) and appends a "No verificado" notice, and logs `iteraciones`/`llamadas_gateway`/`propuestas`/`no_verificados` per turn. **knowledge** `url_es_local` + `is_local_llm_provider/is_generatable(…, ollama_url)` (local host = loopback, private, container or `*.local`; with a public host RAG excludes level >= 2); migration `012_kb_suggestions_creada_por.sql`; `POST /api/v1/knowledge/suggestions/{id}/aceptar` (only whoever generated it, neutral `404`, `409` if already accepted, atomic `rag.aceptada` entry). **Documentation**: the "per-tenant opt-in" promise is withdrawn (main.py, `integraciones-externas` ES/EN, ADR-006) and the guide's "stub" embedding corrected to `local_st`; the `mcp-server/health.py` docstring said it did not install the shared library (it does). `docker-compose*.yml`: `IDENTITY_SIGNING_SECRET` on mcp-server (mandatory in production).
  *Not done and why*: (a) **propagating expediente reclassification to the index (AREA-11-11) does not apply**: `ExpedienteUpdate` does not admit `nivel_seguridad`, no `UPDATE expedientes` touches it and archive-service publishes no events —there is no producer to consume—; `shared/tests/test_el_nivel_del_expediente_no_cambia_en_silencio.py` makes the premise falsifiable. (b) **OMI-04 without the per-entity switch**: setting `knowledge.habilitado` with a `false` default would stop ingestion for current entities; that is a product decision. (c) The per-replica consumer name was already closed by T9. (d) RAG streaming, deferred.
  *Verification*: knowledge 259 passed/55 skipped (314 with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`, real Postgres: atomic acceptance with ownership in the `WHERE`, barrier with a public `OLLAMA_URL` over a real `knowledge_chunk`), mcp-server 260 passed, api-gateway 126 passed, `shared/tests` 249 passed, document-service 682 passed/196 skipped (196 passed with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`); ruff+mypy green on knowledge, mcp-server and api-gateway. Mutations that fall: `is_local_llm_provider` by name (9 unit + 1 integration), dropping `creada_por` from the `WHERE` (2 integration), the entry outside the acceptance transaction (1), `verificar_origen` without checking the signature (4), `idempotency_key` not sent (1), `X-Forwarded-For` not forwarded (1), radicado validation disabled (3), `audit.append` without correlation (1) and an `UPDATE expedientes SET nivel_seguridad` in archive (1). Against the live stack: a radicado confirmed through `POST /mcp/proposals/execute` is recorded with `canal=mcp` and `correlacion.{request_id, idempotency_key=proposal jti}`; a direct call, and one with a forged `X-Origen-Canal`, are `api`; the per-IP bucket of the assistant's internal calls is the client IP's (`X-Forwarded-For: 203.0.113.50` at the edge), not the container's; `POST /knowledge/suggestions/{id}/aceptar`: someone else's `404`, own `200`, repeated `409`, with its entry.
  *Impact*: apply knowledge migration `012` (`python migrations/runner.py`) and deploy knowledge, api-gateway and mcp-server; **channel `mcp` only shows up in services rebuilt with the new shared library** (document-service already is; archive, workflow, signature, notification and the rest will pick it up on their next rebuild, no migration). In production `IDENTITY_SIGNING_SECRET` becomes mandatory for mcp-server too. Entries with channel `mcp` now carry `payload.correlacion`. **Pending**: the invented-radicado check could not be exercised against the real model (the local stack has no `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`); it is covered by tests with a simulated model.
- `fix(shared,mcp,gateway,knowledge,infra)!`: **the mcp-server, the most exposed service, held the MASTER identity secret; and the RAG acceptance entry did not fix WHAT was accepted** (closure of audit findings of batch T10, AREA-11-04/08 and AREA-11-11).
  *Why*: (1) `IDENTITY_SIGNING_SECRET` signs `X-User-Id`/`X-Tenant-Slug` and every service trusts that signature; giving it to the mcp-server (which runs the LLM loop, talks to an external provider and serves external MCP clients) meant that compromising it was enough to impersonate any user of any entity by calling an internal service directly. (2) `kb_suggestions` is mutable and outside the hash chain: the `rag.aceptada` entry only carried `aceptada_en`, so the immutable record could not later prove that the accepted text is the one now in the row.
  *What changes*: **dedicated secret `MCP_ORIGIN_SECRET`**, only on api-gateway and mcp-server, and `IDENTITY_SIGNING_SECRET` **removed** from mcp-server in both compose files (mandatory in production; `infra/.env.example`). `identidad.firmar_origen/verificar_origen` use it; the gateway also signs `X-Real-Ip` into `X-Real-Ip-Signature` (`firmar_ip_real`, bound to IP + person + entity) and `ip_real_firmada` verifies that signature instead of requiring the full identity. With only `MCP_ORIGIN_SECRET` one can mark the channel and verify the IP, but not sign an identity. `rag.aceptada` now carries `sha256_texto` (of the suggested answer), `proveedor`, `modelo` and `fuentes` (the cited `source_ref`s, without their content: Ley 1712), computed from the row locked with `get_for_update` in the same transaction.
  *Not done and why*: **OMI-04 (per-entity `knowledge.habilitado` opt-in)** stays open: implementing it changes visible behaviour (with default `false` it switches ingestion off for current entities) and the user's decision on that default is missing; meanwhile the promise is withdrawn from the docs and ingestion is global. When implemented: the consumer checks it before embedding and `/search`, `/antecedentes` and `/rag` answer a neutral `503`, with a mutation that skips the check.
  *Verification*: knowledge 262 passed/56 skipped (318 with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`, real Postgres), mcp-server 264, api-gateway 128, `shared/tests` 255; ruff+mypy green on knowledge, mcp-server and api-gateway. Mutations that fall: `_secreto_origen` reading the master secret (1), `ip_real_firmada` without comparing the signature (2), IP signature not bound to person/entity (1), the gateway without `firmar_ip_real` (1), mcp-server with the master secret as origin secret (6), entry without `_que_se_acepto` (2) and a fixed-text hash (2). Local stack: mcp-server starts without `IDENTITY_SIGNING_SECRET` and with `MCP_ORIGIN_SECRET`. **Not verified against the live stack**: the assistant's per-IP bucket end to end (needs a real Keycloak token); covered by the gateway and mcp-server tests.
  *Impact*: **set `MCP_ORIGIN_SECRET` in the environment of api-gateway and mcp-server before deploying** (production compose aborts if missing; dev has a default) and rebuild both. Without it the channel falls back to `api` and the IP is not forwarded (safe side). New `rag.aceptada` entries carry the extra fields; earlier ones are not rewritten.
- `fix(workflow,signature,notification,knowledge,shared,gateway)!`: **shared failed-event queue keyed by `event_id` alone silently dropped the second consumer's parking; knowledge could not inspect or replay its queue; fixed consumer names; orphaned dead-letters without a cause; false `X-Truncated`; DLQ size invisible** (audit batch T9, AREA-10-06/07/08/09/10, 13-12). `evento_dead_letter` is one physical table for four consumers: primary key is now `(origin_group, event_id)` (identical idempotent migration in all four services, any order; `ON CONFLICT (origin_group, event_id)` in the four repositories); knowledge gains `/api/v1/knowledge/admin/deadletter` (list, detail, atomic `replay` via `apply_event`, `discard`; `PERM_DLQ_ADMIN`, writes at level 3, no-read-up, neutral `404`) routed by the gateway; `events.consumer_name()` per replica; `record_failure` keeps the last error so orphaned dead-letters carry a cause; `reclaim_stale(status_name=…)` publishes the last sweep and `/health/ready` shows `checks.dead_letter` plus DLQ length (informational, never the `503`); listings ask for `cap + 1` rows so `X-Truncated` only fires beyond the cap. Verified: workflow 355/97 (452 with `RUN_INTEGRATION=1`), signature 503/13 (514), notification 228/19 (247), knowledge 223/43 (266), gateway 112, `shared/tests` 226, lint green; 13 mutations that fail tests; verified live on the local stack.
